# Campaign Overhaul stat tables

## Campaign Overhaul stat tables

Every value the Campaign Overhaul changes, relative to 190 Expanded. In the effect tables, each row is one building, technology, court position, skill or effect bundle. **added** and **removed** mean an effect line was added to or taken away from it. Cells with more than 14 changes are shortened with “… and N more”.

[Back to the overview](#top)
